# Seed lab notebook

This log is append-only. Each entry has: ID · date · hypothesis · command or setup · pass/fail criterion · result · interpretation · decision.
Entries E000–E007 come from the Claude design session, before this repo existed. They were run on a 2-core CPU container, mostly with **32 evaluation worlds** (not EVAL v1's 128), and some used code versions that predate the final v0. Treat them as pilot evidence, not results.

---

## E000 · 2026-11-10 · Pipeline smoke test
- **Setup:** v0 draft (12+12 blobs, energy cost 0.005/step, no efference copy). 6 gens, pop 16, 2 worlds.
- **Result:** Runs end to end, about 2 s/gen on CPU. Survival at end of life was about 0.2 to 0.3.
- **Decision:** Scale up the pilot.

## E001 · 2026-11-10 · Does learning evolve in the first draft?
- **Hypothesis:** ES discovers within-life learning from a blank start.
- **Setup:** Same draft. Pop 32 × 4 worlds. Stopped at gen 30 (of 80).
- **Result:** Fitness noisy around 0. Within-life accuracy about 0.49 → 0.52. Survival about 0.23.
- **Interpretation:** Most agents starved before they had enough eating events to learn from.
- **Decision:** A denser world and a lower energy cost (24+24 blobs, 0.003/step). These are now the v0 defaults.

## E002 · 2026-11-10 · Denser world, blank start
- **Setup:** 24+24 blobs, cost 0.003, no efference copy. 50 gens, pop 32 × 4.
- **Result (32 eval worlds):** evolved 0.23 · frozen 0.42 · random genome 0.67. Accuracy 0.50 → 0.53.
- **Interpretation:** No learning. The evolved genome was not better than random.
- **Decision:** Check whether learning is *reachable* at all with a hand-set learner.

## E003 · 2026-11-10 · Is learning reachable? (hand-set dopamine-style rule) ✔
- **Finding:** Motor neurons held tanh activity, not the action actually sampled, so reward-gated Hebbian updates strengthened the wrong synapses. **Fix:** an efference copy (motor neurons set to one-hot of the chosen action). This is now in v0.
- **Setup:** Genome with only the motor←sensor rule A = 1, modulator weight on energy change 3, log plasticity rate −1, motor←sensor wiring logit 0. This is now `biology_prior()`.
- **Result (32 worlds):** fitness **6.32**, accuracy **0.58 → 0.69**, survival 0.64 · frozen 1.08 · random −0.15.
- **Interpretation:** The architecture can learn within a life, and the learning does the work.

## E004 · 2026-11-10 · Blank start with efference copy
- **Setup:** log plasticity rate init −3, ES lr 0.03. 50 gens, pop 32 × 4.
- **Result (32 worlds):** 0.59, accuracy 0.51 → 0.53 · frozen 0.27.
- **Interpretation:** Still nothing. With an Adam step of about 0.03 per gen, the plasticity rate needs about 70 gens just to reach a useful range.
- **Decision:** Raise the initial plasticity rate and ES lr.

## E005 · 2026-11-10 · Blank start, log plasticity rate init −1.5, ES lr 0.1
- **Setup:** 50 gens, pop 32 × 4. These are the current v0 defaults.
- **Result (32 worlds):** **0.93**, accuracy 0.52 → 0.54 · frozen 0.66.
- **Interpretation:** At most a hint, within noise. **The main open question: does a blank start discover learning at larger scale?** (Experiment A.)

## E006 · 2026-11-10 · Biology start, σ 0.1, lr 0.1
- **Setup:** Mean initialised to `biology_prior`. 30 gens, pop 32 × 4.
- **Result (32 worlds):** **4.73**, accuracy 0.58 → 0.64 · frozen **0.07**. In a separate 5-gen quick run: 6.3 → 1.5.
- **Interpretation:** Plasticity carries the evolved brain entirely (the lesion collapses it), but large mutations degrade the prior.

## E007 · 2026-11-10 · Biology start, gentle ES (σ 0.05, lr 0.02)
- **Setup:** 20 gens, pop 32 × 4.
- **Result (32 worlds):** **6.27**, accuracy 0.59 → 0.70.
- **Interpretation:** Gentle settings preserve the prior. Whether evolution can *improve* it is open (Experiment B).

## E008 · 2026-11-12 · Repo created, runner refactor
- `evolve` was split into `es_init` / `es_step` for checkpoint and resume. Rerunning E007 reproduced its numbers exactly (6.265625, accuracy 0.594 → 0.702), so the refactor did not change behaviour.

## E101 · 2026-11-12 · Preregistered pipeline smoke test
- **Hypothesis:** The committed v0 runner executes end to end in the laptop's local Python environment.
- **Exact command (PowerShell, repository root):**
  ```powershell
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E101-smoke --quick
  ```
- **Setup:** Runner quick settings: 3 generations, population 8, 2 training worlds, 4 evaluation worlds, checkpoint every generation. Run only after committing the code and this preregistration. No `--allow-dirty` is planned.
- **Pass:** Process completes successfully, `summary.json` reports `finished` at generation 3, and all reported evaluation metrics are finite.
- **Fail:** Crash, incomplete run, or non-finite evaluation metrics. Keep any failed run folder and diagnose before scaling.
- **Result:** Pending. This is an operational check; its four-world scores will not support an EVAL v1 claim.
- **Decision after recording:** If it passes, run E102. Regenerate the results table with `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/summarize.py` and append the observed outcome here.

## E102 · 2026-11-12 · Preregistered full-settings benchmark
- **Hypothesis:** The default population and training-world count can run within the laptop's compute and storage limits.
- **Exact command (PowerShell, repository root):**
  ```powershell
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E102-benchmark --generations 10 --seed 0
  ```
- **Setup:** Population 128, 8 training worlds, default world/brain configuration, 128 evaluation worlds, checkpoint every 10 generations. One low-priority compute job at a time. Use the supported Windows process-priority mechanism rather than the Unix `nice` command.
- **Pass:** Run finishes with finite metrics within 3 hours. Record total wall time, first-generation compilation cost, subsequent generation times from `metrics.jsonl`, and evaluation overhead. Use those measurements to estimate whether each proposed 200-generation run fits under 3 hours and whether the day's total stays under about 8 hours.
- **Fail:** Crash, non-finite metrics, or inability to finish within the per-run limit. Stop before exceeding the limit, retain artifacts, and use the measured constraint to choose a supported resumable schedule before longer runs.
- **Result:** Pending. A single-seed benchmark is not a claim that evolution learns.
- **Decision after recording:** Proceed to E100 reference measurements if the environment is viable. Schedule core experiments only after their wall-time and daily-compute estimates are known. Regenerate the results table and append the measured outcome.

## E100 · 2026-11-12 · Preregistered EVAL v1 reference measurements
- **Hypothesis:** The biology prior shows within-life learning and a positive plasticity lesion effect under the fixed 128-world EVAL v1 protocol; random initial genomes provide the comparison reference.
- **Exact commands (PowerShell, repository root; execute sequentially):**
  ```powershell
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E100-blank-s0 --start blank --generations 0 --seed 0
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E100-blank-s1 --start blank --generations 0 --seed 1
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E100-blank-s2 --start blank --generations 0 --seed 2
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E100-biology-s0 --start biology --generations 0 --seed 0
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E100-biology-s1 --start biology --generations 0 --seed 1
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E100-biology-s2 --start biology --generations 0 --seed 2
  ```
- **Setup:** No evolution and no configuration overrides. All final-learning and frozen evaluations retain `test_genome`'s seed 123 and the runner's default 128 worlds. Confirm the zero-generation runner path preserves the starting genome. Do not change the evaluation protocol to manufacture independent repeats.
- **Measurement-completion pass:** All six tracked runs finish with finite metrics and the expected unchanged start/final genome. Copy learning fitness, frozen fitness, first-half accuracy, and second-half accuracy from each `summary.json`; compute accuracy gain and lesion gap transparently from those values.
- **Hypothesis pass:** The biology reference has positive second-minus-first-half accuracy and learning-minus-frozen fitness. Failure of either condition means this reproduction hypothesis fails. Report random-reference variation across seeds descriptively; do not infer an evolutionary improvement from zero-generation runs.
- **Independence limitation:** Before interpreting repetitions, inspect genome hashes and the implementation's seed use. If biology initialization is deterministic, its three zero-generation runs under fixed evaluation seed 123 are duplicate reproducibility checks, not three independent scientific trials. They cannot establish a three-seed evolutionary claim. The later A/B/C comparisons still require three evolution seeds each.
- **Result:** Pending. Pilot values from 32 worlds are not substituted for these measurements.
- **Decision after recording:** Append results, interpretation, and the next experimental decision; regenerate `results/RESULTS.md`. Consider the EVAL v1 milestone only after these measurements and their limitations are recorded. Core A/B/C experiments remain governed by HANDOFF §5 and the measured compute budget.

## E103–E105 · 2026-11-12 · Preregistered core comparisons
- **Scheduling condition:** Start only after E102's measured throughput projects a 200-generation run, including evaluation, below 3 hours. Otherwise implement and test graceful checkpoint-boundary stopping before scheduling the same scientific settings in bounded segments. One low-priority compute job at a time; checkpoint every 10 generations. Complete E100 references before judging these comparisons.
- **Shared protocol:** Default world/brain configuration, population 128, 8 training worlds, 200 generations, evolution seeds 0/1/2, and unchanged EVAL v1 (128 worlds, evaluation seed 123). Commit this preregistration and source before launching. Preserve every run and record full scores and gaps from summaries; no tuning on evaluation worlds.

### E103 · A: can blank-start evolution discover learning?
- **Hypothesis:** Evolution from a blank start produces within-life learning that relies on synaptic plasticity in at least 2 of 3 evolution seeds.
- **Exact commands (PowerShell, repository root):**
  ```powershell
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E103-A-blank-s0 --start blank --generations 200 --seed 0
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E103-A-blank-s1 --start blank --generations 200 --seed 1
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E103-A-blank-s2 --start blank --generations 200 --seed 2
  ```
- **Gate:** In at least 2 of 3 seeds, final-learning second-minus-first-half accuracy is strictly greater than 0.03 **and** frozen fitness is clearly below learning fitness, preserving HANDOFF's criterion. Report each full learning-minus-frozen gap. HANDOFF supplies no numerical lesion cutoff; a tiny positive gap alone does not establish a clear effect. Qualify ambiguous effects without inventing statistical significance or advancing the roadmap from an ambiguous gate. If fewer than 2 seeds meet both requirements, the gate is not established.
- **Result:** Pending. If the gate fails, follow HANDOFF's one-change-at-a-time sequence, beginning with more training worlds; preregister any subsequent experiment separately.

### E104 · B: can evolution improve the biology prior?
- **Hypothesis:** Gentle ES improves on the E100 biology-prior learning fitness in at least 2 of 3 evolution seeds.
- **Exact commands (PowerShell, repository root):**
  ```powershell
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E104-B-bio-s0 --start biology --sigma 0.05 --lr 0.02 --generations 200 --seed 0
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E104-B-bio-s1 --start biology --sigma 0.05 --lr 0.02 --generations 200 --seed 1
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E104-B-bio-s2 --start biology --sigma 0.05 --lr 0.02 --generations 200 --seed 2
  ```
- **Gate:** Final-learning fitness strictly exceeds the E100 biology-prior learning fitness under EVAL v1 in at least 2 of 3 seeds. Otherwise this gate fails. Record per-seed differences, within-life accuracy, and lesion gaps; do not describe a merely preserved prior as improved.
- **Result:** Pending.

### E105 · C: evolution with synaptic plasticity disabled
- **Hypothesis:** The no-plasticity evolutionary control provides a baseline below the learning performance of A and B; A versus C is the matched intervention comparison.
- **Exact commands (PowerShell, repository root):**
  ```powershell
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E105-C-noplastic-s0 --start blank --no-plastic --generations 200 --seed 0
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E105-C-noplastic-s1 --start blank --no-plastic --generations 200 --seed 1
  .\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E105-C-noplastic-s2 --start blank --no-plastic --generations 200 --seed 2
  ```
- **Comparison:** C's actual control performance is `final_frozen`; compare it with A/B `final_learning` by paired evolution seed and report all differences. Verify A/C starting-genome hashes match for each paired seed, along with settings other than the plasticity switch. C's `final_learning` re-enables plasticity and is diagnostic only.
- **Interpretation:** This is a baseline, not a separate roadmap gate. Positive A-minus-C differences support the proposed plasticity advantage; zero or negative differences count against it and must be reported. B versus C also changes the starting prior and ES hyperparameters, so it cannot isolate plasticity's causal effect. Do not convert this comparison into an unsupported significance claim.
- **Result:** Pending. After each completed experiment, regenerate the results table, append its results and interpretation, and commit/push the run artifacts with the record. Gate decisions require the planned three seeds and explicit treatment of uncertainty.

## E103–E105 scheduling amendment and resource checkpoint · 2026-11-12
- **Amendment to the preceding core commands:** Append `--max-train-seconds 7200` to each of the nine initial E103/E104/E105 commands. If a run pauses before generation 200, resume that same folder with `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --resume <run-folder> --max-train-seconds 7200`. The saved target remains 200; this changes scheduling only, not scientific settings or EVAL v1.

The coordinator reviewed the change; it is not yet committed. Commit it before using the new option. E102 continues under its original `8545333` code.
- **Limit:** The 7,200-second training budget excludes setup and evaluation and may overrun by one generation. Monitor full wall time separately to preserve the under-3-hour per-process limit; this option alone is not a hard wall-time guarantee.
- **Resource snapshot:** C: 6.94 GiB free, down from the recent 10.46 GiB reading. The pagefile allocation was 8,618 MiB, but there is no causal baseline establishing what changed it; do not attribute the free-space drop to the pagefile. The active benchmark had about 3.25 GiB private memory and 2.69 GiB resident memory. E102 had reached generation 7 of 10; final results remain pending.
- **Decision and process lesson:** Launch no additional heavy work until free-space stability and headroom are checked. Resource measurements must govern scheduling alongside runtime estimates; distinguish observed disk/RAM readings from unverified explanations. Record the completed benchmark at its next meaningful checkpoint.

The coordinator verified checkpoint `t=0`, exact equality of start/final/checkpoint mean arrays and hashes, and `start_learning == final_learning` in every run. - **Interpretation:** The reference reproduction hypothesis passes: the biology prior gains about 10.54 percentage points in within-life food-choice accuracy and loses 5.12890625 fitness when synaptic plasticity is disabled. Its three runs have the same genome and scores, so they are deterministic repeatability checks, not three independent scientific trials. Blank genomes have three distinct hashes; their identical frozen scores are expected because seed-dependent initial Hebbian-rule variation is disabled by the lesion. This measures available starting behavior, not evolution's ability to discover or improve learning.
- **Recording:** Regenerated and checked all eight rows in `results/RESULTS.md`; updated README from pilot numbers to these 128-world references. Per-run storage guards were C: at least 5 GiB and D: at least 10 GiB before launch, with one `BelowNormal` compute job at a time and temporary files on D:.

## E105 C seed 0 result · 2026-11-14 · First matched control trails plastic evolution
- **Evidence:** `runs/20261112-224133_E105-C-noplastic-s0/summary.json` reports `finished`, generation 200, 128 evaluation worlds, and final hash `37f801f0906a6d3b`. | Evaluation | Fitness | Accuracy first | Accuracy second | Survival | Eaten |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Actual C control (`final_frozen`) | 0.486328125 | 0.50419020652771 | 0.5398915410041809 | 0.408203125 | 7713 |
| Plasticity re-enabled diagnostic (`final_learning`) | 0.0625 | 0.5028968453407288 | 0.5004035234451294 | 0.37890625 | 7656 |
| Start learning (`start_learning`) | -0.00390625 | 0.5008518099784851 | 0.49779558181762695 | 0.3828125 | 7778 |

- **Matched comparison:** A0 `final_learning` fitness 16.591796875 minus C0 `final_frozen` fitness 0.486328125 is **16.10546875**, favoring evolution with plasticity in this first matched pair. The 0.0625 re-enabled score is diagnostic and is not C's control score. C1/C2 remain; one seed does not establish a replicated A-versus-C conclusion. C has no new numerical gate, and no tag or roadmap advancement follows.
- **Accuracy limitation observed:** C0 frozen second-minus-first-half accuracy is **0.03570133447647095** (3.57 percentage points), above 0.03 even with local predictor/Hebbian updates disabled. Recurrent state, energy feedback, and survival/encounter selection remain possible explanations; this result does not isolate which produced the gain. ## E106 · 2026-11-14 · EVAL v2: separate predictor and Hebbian contributions

- **Authorization and timing:** The owner explicitly approved EVAL v2 after the C controls: evaluate fixed saved A0/A1/A2 and existing references under Both, Hebbian-only, and Frozen, without new evolution or changed worlds. Complete and verify C2 first. EVAL v1 remains intact; B1/B2 remain planned. This is a diagnostic extension, not a roadmap gate.
- **Question and hypotheses:** With each saved genome and its evaluation randomness held fixed, does adding predictor learning to Hebbian learning improve fitness, and does Hebbian learning without predictor learning improve fitness over frozen synapses? For each genome, `Both - Hebbian-only > 0` supports a positive predictor contribution conditional on Hebbian learning; zero or negative differences count against that positive contribution. `Hebbian-only - Frozen > 0` supports a positive Hebbian contribution with predictor learning disabled; zero or negative differences count against it. These are observed contributions in this architecture and toy domain, not universal necessity claims.
- **Fixed panel:** Seven saved genomes: three independently evolved A genomes, three unique blank references, and one unique biology reference. Each run has zero evolutionary generations, default `Cfg`, 128 evaluation worlds, and evaluation seed 123. CLI seeds 0/1/2 record input provenance and do not change the fixed evaluation seed. All three conditions use matched world, development, and action random keys. Do not tune genomes, conditions, thresholds, or worlds from these results.
- **Conditions and outputs:** Both uses `plastic=True, predictor=True`; Hebbian-only uses `plastic=True, predictor=False`; Frozen uses `plastic=False, predictor=False`. Each condition records all five existing metrics: fitness, first-half accuracy, second-half accuracy, survival, and eaten count. New config records the source file and its full binary SHA-256; summary explicitly identifies EVAL v2 and the condition labels. Old EVAL v1 results and run files remain unchanged.

### Validation, interpretation, and budget

- **Implementation checks before execution:** Preserve default EVAL v1 behavior; test explicit protocol/condition dispatch and labels, independent predictor disabling, and the behavioral invariant that predictor-only with Hebbian updates off matches Frozen. Rebuild the notebook if the engine changes. No diagnostic implementation is applied before C2 completes.
- **Operational pass:** All seven panels complete with finite metrics; their saved input genomes remain byte-identical; Both and Frozen exactly reproduce all corresponding saved EVAL v1 final-learning and final-frozen metrics. A mismatch or nonfinite result fails validation: preserve the artifacts and halt scientific interpretation until the cause is resolved. Do not silently substitute a tolerance or overwrite previous results to pass this check.
- **Report:** Publish every per-genome result and both signed fitness differences, including zero, negative, and mixed outcomes. Report the pattern across all three evolved A seeds only after all three are complete, then include all references in the completed seven-panel account. Also report the continuous retained-benefit ratio `(Hebbian-only - Frozen) / (Both - Frozen)` only when the denominator is positive; otherwise mark it undefined. Do not clip ratios, add a post-hoc “most benefit” threshold, invent a new gate, or claim statistical significance from this small fixed panel.
- **Scientific limits:** This tests predictor learning, not removal of the predictor's static signal or all possible prediction mechanisms. Hebbian-only retains the rest of the architecture, including recurrence, energy feedback, and the modulation pathway. Pooled food-choice accuracy can reflect encounter and survival selection; accuracy gain alone does not establish a learning mechanism. ## E107 · 2026-11-14 · Bounded lifetime structural plasticity: capability, benchmark, paired diagnostic

- **Authorization and ownership:** The owner requested implementation and direct measurement of activity-dependent connection addition/pruning among existing neurons. This lab works only in the isolated `.tmp/parallel-labs/structural` checkout based on `a0cb4a7`; its remote push URL remains disabled. Main integration and publication are coordinated separately. E106's predictor switch remains an independent change to combine, not replace. Preserve C2/E106/B1/B2 and all old artifacts. No new neurons, genes, environment/reward changes, evaluation-world tuning, or novelty/biological-fidelity/AGI claim.
- **Versioned rule, fixed before measurement:** `activity-grow-prune-v1`, default off. Every tenth zero-based step starting at 10 (10..290 for a default life), after the brain tick, use the previous activity to prune at most one inactive edge per row whose absolute weight is at most `0.01*wmax`, weakest then lowest presynaptic index. Independently add at most one previously absent legal edge with the greatest positive absolute coactivity, lowest-index ties, using absolute presynaptic activity for a row empty after pruning. An edge pruned in this event cannot be re-added immediately. Sensors receive no incoming edges; no self-edges. Each row's immutable cap is `min(legal_candidates, birth_degree + 1)`, so net growth is bounded by at most `H+6` edges. New signed weights use the unchanged genome's birth scale and expected birth fan-in, clipped to `wmax`; a `fold_in` stream tag 107 and event index preserve world/action RNG. Exactly zero proposals skip growth; nonfinite proposals/scales cause an explicit validation failure at evaluation/ES boundaries. Frozen and dead agents do not rewire. The current action is already computed, so structure changes affect the next neural tick.
- **Software acceptance:** Direct fixtures must separately show `+1` edge/nonzero weight, `-1` edge/zero weight, an initially isolated hidden target becoming active only on a later tick, and a changed selected edge when input activity changes. Verify legal masks, fixed caps, weight bounds, deterministic ties, full/empty rows, dead/frozen/disabled/nonevent no-ops, zero/nonfinite proposals, and actual mask-difference counters. Compare disabled current/default rollouts bitwise with base `a0cb4a7`, including trajectories and instrumented internal W/x/Wp/key/mask, preserve positional calls and 155 default parameters, and verify tiny real structural ES split/resume plus flushed-tail crash/replay exactly. Unit fixtures use temporary roots under `.tmp`, not research runs.
- **Direct software command:** `& 'D:\cool-projects\general-intelligence\.venv\Scripts\python.exe' -X utf8 -m unittest discover -s tests -v` from the isolated lab, under a BelowNormal five-minute supervisor with D: temporary files and a 5-GiB available-commit stop. This is direct software testing, not a scientific claim. Initial baseline: eight runner tests passed. Ten kernel tests and four integration tests passed before the final runner changes. A combined run exposed five legacy recovery failures caused by an overly broad validation exception handler; the handler was narrowed to `StructuralValidationError` without weakening the original recovery assertions. Final full suite and added real-ES check pending the coordinator's brief compute hold. Preserve failed fixture console files in `.tmp`.

## E109 · 2026-11-14 · Benchmark evolution with the existing structural rule

- **Rationale and scope:** E107's fixed-genome intervention reduced fitness in all three saved A genomes (Weight+structure minus Weight-only: -2.591796875, -1.423828125, -0.5078125). Those genomes evolved without structural changes. E109 asks the different question of whether evolution can adapt to the existing structural dynamics. Its **first task is full-population resource/timing feasibility**, using a useful, resumable training segment. It is not a learning claim, a rule search, or an automatic commitment to finish 200 generations or launch three seeds.
- **Unchanged intervention:** `activity-grow-prune-v1`, interval 10, prune fraction 0.01, extra fan-in 1, structural stream tag 107, and existing post-brain-tick timing. Both Hebbian and predictor updates stay enabled. Do not add rules, tune thresholds, change the world, alter the 155-number genome, or use evaluation seed 123 to choose changes. B1/B2 remain planned; rule reversal is a separate later approval/protocol question.

### Exact first command

Commit this registration and the reviewed integrated code first, then run only through the existing runner, under the coordinator's guarded low-priority launcher:

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --name E109-structural-train-s0 --start blank --seed 0 --generations 200 --pop 128 --worlds 8 --sigma 0.1 --lr 0.1 --eval-worlds 128 --eval-protocol structural-v1 --structural-plasticity activity-grow-prune-v1 --checkpoint-every 10 --max-train-seconds 600
```

The current runner and source worker confirm that `structural-v1` permits nonzero-generation blank-start training. EVAL v2 does not. No new protocol or runner option is required for this command. `--no-plastic`, `--quick`, `--allow-dirty`, and configuration overrides are absent.

- **Matched settings/provenance:** Seed 0, default `Cfg`, population 128, eight training worlds, sigma/lr 0.1/0.1, 200-generation target and checkpoint interval 10 match A0. Verify the new `genome_start.npy` equals `runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0/genome_start.npy` byte-for-byte and its short array hash is `9a761b031011f9ee`. Record the actual clean main source commit, package versions, full saved settings, and structural rule metadata. The only scientific training intervention is enabled structural dynamics; scheduling and explicit structural evaluation/reporting differ deliberately. Do not substitute an old lab source hash for the actual integrated source.
- **Evaluation boundary:** The scheduled first segment should stop before the target and perform no final evaluation. The runner evaluates only when generation 200 is reached. If later completed, existing `structural-v1` reports Weight-only, Weight+structure, and Frozen on the same 128 worlds/seed 123, with the existing frozen semantics. Do not invoke intermediate held-out evaluations or choose continuation from their scores.

### Budget, operational criteria, and next decision

- **Guarded execution:** One full-population heavy job, `BelowNormal`, temporary files on D:, coordinate with peer labs before launch. Startup floors remain C:/D: 10 GiB each, physical memory 8 GiB, available commit 10 GiB. Runtime floors remain C: 5 GiB, D: 10 GiB, available commit 3 GiB, checked about every ten seconds. Preserve the existing owned-process stop/verification path and 10,500-second full-wall limit, below three hours. Browsers remain untouched.
- **600 seconds is a training scheduling limit, not a hard ten-minute wall limit.** It is checked between complete generations, so it may overrun by one generation. The loop timer includes first-generation compilation and any resume replay; it excludes earlier setup and final evaluation. Record supervisor wall time and resource samples separately. Split first-generation seconds from subsequent generation timings; report warm count, median, and range only when subsequent rows exist. Do not present cold-start or evaluation time as steady training throughput. With no warm row, throughput estimation remains incomplete.
- **Operational pass:** At least one full-size generation completes; checkpoint arrays and all metric values are finite; saved generation and contiguous history agree at the normal stop; all structural invalid-proposal/cap/illegal-edge/invalid-weight checks remain zero; initialization/settings match A0; resource floors hold and the process stops normally with a resumable checkpoint. Record actual edge-change diagnostics, timings, and sampled memory; a normal scheduled `interrupted` status is expected and is not a scientific failure.
- **Operational fail/stop:** OOM, resource-floor or full-wall stop, structural validation failure, nonfinite state, provenance/settings mismatch, or inconsistent checkpoint/history fails this feasibility attempt. Preserve its entire folder, logs, and any flushed tail. If killed between checkpoints, verify replay before appending; never edit old rows. Do not silently reduce population/worlds, change the rule, or restart under a new name to erase the failure.
- **Decision after benchmark:** Preserve and publish the segment with its resource/timing result. If feasible, use measured warm timing and available headroom to decide a bounded continuation of the **same folder and target**, recording its new time budget before resuming. If timings are insufficient, another bounded segment is a measurement decision, not evidence of learning. Any projection to generation 200 must state its warm-sample count and exclude future compilation/evaluation overhead rather than promise a completion time. A failed benchmark calls for a documented operational decision before more compute.

Resume syntax, only after that decision and a clean preservation commit:

```powershell
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_experiment.py --resume runs/<recorded-E109-folder> --max-train-seconds <recorded-next-budget>
```

- **Scientific reporting:** Early training fitness and one seed cannot establish improvement. A later A-matched structural-evolution comparison needs its exact three-seed scope and criteria preregistered before expansion; report all paired differences and compute costs, not only favorable seeds. Distinguish adaptation under structural dynamics from a claim that adaptive rewiring beats a capacity-matched static network. This first segment produces an operational feasibility decision only; no learning gate, milestone tag, general-intelligence claim, or state-of-the-art claim follows.
- **Result:** Pending. No benchmark was launched while preparing this draft.

### E108 interpretation and next decision

- The preregistered retention hypothesis is supported in all four shifts, with positive
  advantages in 3/3 A seeds per condition (the criterion required >=2/3). This is a
  completed descriptive three-seed result, not a new roadmap gate or significance test.
  Sparse resources reduce mean advantage from 15.048177083333334 to 5.069661458333333;
  dense resources increase it to 19.085286458333332. High cost retains mean advantage
  13.702473958333334; low cost 15.1484375. Report the raw per-seed differences above.
- Blank references are mixed near zero (range -0.75390625 to +0.40625), including eight
  negative advantages among 15 conditions. The unique biology prior retains positive
  advantage in all five conditions (2.412109375 to 8.55859375). Thus parameter robustness
  is not unique to evolved A; the prior also retains it, at smaller observed magnitudes.
- These are shifts within the same task family, fixed saved genomes and jointly
  enabled/disabled Hebbian/predictor updates. Pooled accuracy, encounter selection,
  recurrent state and energy feedback limit interpretation. No individual recovery,
  new-task transfer, SOTA, universal necessity, or AGI claim follows. EVAL v1 worlds
  were not changed or used for tuning, and BROADER final results caused no tuning.
- Final audit verified all 350 raw metric values, 35 matched condition pairs, unchanged
  source/start/final bytes, exact condition differences, clean source commits and all
  successful guard exits. Regenerated RESULTS has 15 unchanged historical rows plus
  40 broader rows (five development, 35 final). README now records the completed result.
  The report formatter initially used a wrong local record key; it was corrected and
  re-executed read-only, without rerunning or editing any scientific run.
- Pin and commit the complete E108 artifacts/provenance locally with push disabled
  before further edits. The coordinator requested an executable food-rule-reversal
  prototype next, on a separate branch preserving E108: no-flip versus unannounced
  flip at step150 in T300, matched keys, per-agent/window/recovery and survival/encounter
  accounting. Software/direct fixtures are authorized; a new scientific protocol needs
  concrete versioned registration and owner approval before final-world execution.

## E111 preregistration · 2026-11-18 · One cold generation: laptop CPU versus free Colab T4
- **Question and scope:** Does the actual free Colab GPU deployment complete one cold, fully synchronized generation at least **five times faster** than the laptop deployment? This is the owner's Phase 0 operational benchmark, not a learning/generalization experiment or a three-seed scientific claim. The ignored, unregistered E110 proposal remains superseded; E111 is a distinct benchmark.
- **Measurement correction before execution:** Add only `jax.block_until_ready(state)` after `es_step` and before stopping the runner's generation timer. No engine, random keys, parameters, world or evaluation protocol changed. Reference: [JAX asynchronous dispatch](https://docs.jax.dev/en/latest/async_dispatch.html). - **Fixed comparison:** One new CPU run, then one GPU run, from the same clean published source commit. Each uses the identical saved biology-prior bytes `runs/20261112-131946_E104-B-bio-s0/genome_start.npy` (runner hash **72caddf0cdd93943**), seed **2**, population **128**, **8** worlds, sigma **0.05**, learning rate **0.02**, default Cfg and both lifetime updates. - **Exact GPU command:** `/content/seed-benchmark-venv/bin/python -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --name E111-benchmark-gpu --start file:runs/20261112-131946_E104-B-bio-s0/genome_start.npy --sigma 0.05 --lr 0.02 --generations 1 --seed 2 --pop 128 --worlds 8 --eval-worlds 128 --checkpoint-every 10 --max-train-seconds 300`, from the private bundle checkout. Both run in fresh processes with `JAX_ENABLE_COMPILATION_CACHE=false`, `JAX_LOG_COMPILES=1`, `XLA_PYTHON_CLIENT_PREALLOCATE=false` and UTF-8. CPU forces `JAX_PLATFORMS=cpu`; GPU forces `JAX_PLATFORMS=cuda`, with a separate subprocess asserting actual GPU devices/backend and the pinned core versions before the run. No CPU fallback or `--allow-dirty` is permitted.

No global Colab package mutation, laptop dependency installation, credentials, token export, paid runtime or repository-visibility change is needed.
- **Timing and decision fixed now:** Primary metric is the synchronized **cold** `metrics.jsonl` generation `seconds`, including first-use compilation within that step. Report CPU and GPU raw seconds and **CPU/GPU ratio**. Record supervisor full-process wall time, directly measured setup/preflight timing and raw compilation logs separately. ## E112 preregistration · 2026-11-19 · Phase 1 EVAL v3 diagnostics
- **Question and hypothesis:** On fresh worlds, do the saved evolved A genomes improve food-choice accuracy with meal experience, and how much does performance deteriorate after an unannounced reversal? Measure rather than assume the external audit's findings. The owner's request to keep progressing and long-term ambition do not establish a capability or remove the existing phase gates, resource limits or evidence requirements.
- **Scope:** Saved A0/A1/A2, B0, one saved biology-prior reference, and all three saved E100 blank references. No evolution, fitting, reward change, new mechanism or genome selection. The biology-prior genome is deterministic, so repeat copies are not independent seeds. B0 is a descriptive single-seed diagnostic, never a three-seed result. EVAL v1 and all old artifacts remain unchanged.
- **Worlds fixed before evaluation:** EVAL v3 uses default Cfg, 256 worlds, seed **2026111901**, alternating initial rules (128 each). Split one PRNG key into world/development streams exactly as existing evaluators; precompute all world keys before batching in groups of 16 to limit memory. Use those identical keys/rules for every saved genome and reference policy, stationary and reversal. Reversal switches the hidden food rule before eating at step index 150, with no cue to the genome. World trajectories can diverge after different actions; matching worlds means matching initial state and random streams, not forced identical food locations throughout life. Development uses only seeds 41/42 and tiny synthetic fixtures, never the held-out seed or seed 123/777 for tuning.
- **Policies fixed now:** Random walk samples uniformly from the same five movement actions, with zero communication. Greedy has the same local visual patch, plus privileged knowledge of the current food rule, updated immediately at reversal. It chooses uniformly among actions minimizing Manhattan distance to visible food, avoiding an immediately poisonous destination whenever a non-poison alternative exists; with no visible food it moves uniformly among safe actions. Reuse the engine's movement, energy, death, simultaneous eating, item regrowth and PRNG schedule. This is a local rule-knowing reference, not an optimal policy or a bound on attainable fitness. Do not tune it to match the external audit's approximately 92 score.
- **Measurements:** For each genome, random walk and greedy in both conditions, retain binary food/poison/alive-before/alive-after arrays by world, step and agent, world keys/rules and per-world fitness. Fitness is mean food-minus-poison per initial agent; its SE is sample SD of the 256 world-family means divided by sqrt(256), never an agent-iid SE. Report fitness divided by the matched greedy mean and a paired world-level delta-method SE; undefined denominators produce null, never zero or infinity. Report ordinal meal accuracy at 1,2,3,5,10,20 with food/encounter counts and the number of agents reaching each ordinal. Each agent contributes at most one event per ordinal; agents who never reach it are excluded and their missingness is explicit. Report 50-step accuracy bins for agents alive at lifetime end, plus all-agent bins and the three post-reversal bins [150,200), [200,250), [250,300). Accuracy SEs use world-cluster ratio residuals, retaining worlds with zero encounters; no encounters gives null. Survivor-conditioned and later-meal curves have selection bias and cannot alone prove each individual improves. Preserve all denominators and raw arrays for auditing.
- **Validity gate:** PASS only when all eight saved-genome panels finish with finite raw fitness/SE, complete requested outputs, validated binary/survival/count accounting, identical registered source/config/key provenance, and unchanged genome input bytes. Null accuracy is valid only for zero qualifying encounters; disclose it. Wrong source/config, missing artifacts, inconsistent accounting, timeout/OOM or guard stop is a failed/incomplete measurement attempt, preserved and reported before proceeding. Phase 1 is a diagnostic, not a new fitness target: a flat/negative learning curve or poor reversal recovery is reported plainly, without retuning or adding a mechanism. Do not claim an effect from a difference within two reported SEs; paired contrasts, if reported, use paired world-cluster uncertainty. Three A seeds are shown individually, not pooled as 768 independent worlds from different populations.
- **Exact commands:** Run each command below sequentially from the repository, after committing implementation and preregistration. No --allow-dirty. Python is `.venv/Scripts/python.exe`; all commands use `-X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol v3 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256`, followed by the listed name and saved input:
  - `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol v3 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --name E112-v3-A0 --start file:runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0/genome_final.npy`
  - `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol v3 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --name E112-v3-A1 --start file:runs/20261112-050319_E103-A-blank-s1/genome_final.npy`
  - `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol v3 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --name E112-v3-A2 --start file:runs/20261112-102755_E103-A-blank-s2/genome_final.npy`
  - `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol v3 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --name E112-v3-B0 --start file:runs/20261112-131946_E104-B-bio-s0/genome_final.npy`
  - `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol v3 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --name E112-v3-biology --start file:runs/20261112-022641_E100-biology-s0/genome_start.npy`
  - `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol v3 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --name E112-v3-blank0 --start file:runs/20261112-022439_E100-blank-s0/genome_start.npy`
  - `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol v3 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --name E112-v3-blank1 --start file:runs/20261112-022514_E100-blank-s1/genome_start.npy`

- **Interpretation:** All three evolved A genomes improve first-to-twentieth-meal accuracy within the cohort reaching20: 26.8170±2.4210,21.1392±2.4399,24.8718±2.2402 percentage points (SE). This supports within-life learning in these stationary worlds. After reversal, first postflip accuracy is 25.6–28.0%; final-bin accuracy is 46.2–49.3%, with detailed SEs and denominators in `results/EVAL_V3.md`. Stationary fitness is only18.2–18.7% of the local rule-knowing greedy score; reversal fitness is4.5–5.2%. Learning is real within this test; reliable reversal adaptation is not demonstrated.
- **Limits:** All uncertainty is across world-families, not across evolution seeds. Later-meal and final-survivor curves condition on selection; even matched gains concern only agents reaching20. B0 is a single-seed diagnostic. Greedy has privileged rule knowledge and is not an optimal upper bound. This diagnostic uses weight plasticity with structural growth off; it does not show new-edge formation, a wiring-prior benefit or a fair nonplastic-training advantage. The external audit's hidden-layer/modulator lesions were not remeasured by this listed Phase1 protocol and remain unverified here.
- **Decision/next:** Phase1's measurement gate passes; weak reversal is a finding, not hidden by a changed threshold. Publish all raw panels, summary/report/README/LOG together and tag the milestone. Proceed to Phase2: preregister a fair directly encoded recurrent controller with no lifetime weight changes, then compare its three matched-budget evolution seeds to A. No speculative new biological mechanism is added before that control.

## E113 preregistration · 2026-11-19 · Phase 2 directly encoded recurrent baseline
- **Question/hypothesis:** Can an independently trained recurrent controller with fixed lifetime weights match or exceed the plastic A agents under the same outer ES budget? A recurrent state can carry adaptation without synaptic changes; see [RL²](https://arxiv.org/abs/1611.02779). This is the owner's Phase2 baseline, not the historical tied-sensor C control and not a new plasticity mechanism. Phase1 measurement gate is decided and published at594acfe1ff75ced3bbc26a7a91645ff64bdcd203, milestone v0.2-evalv3.
- **Council decision before training:** Literature research, implementation review and skeptical review support retaining recurrence and independent sensory-channel weights. The skeptical review rejected a two-of-three continuation exception because Phase2 did not specify one; use the conservative all-three decision below. Review also found ordinary brain_step clips weights even when plasticity is disabled. The direct path must bypass all synaptic/predictor updates and clipping, preserving fixed weights exactly, including a fixture above3. Numerical biology-inspired mechanisms remain deferred to their registered failed-rung decisions.
- **Controller format:** `direct-rnn-v1` directly encodes every off-diagonal weight into a non-sensor neuron, row-major post then pre. Default dimensions remain 103 sensor values,48 hidden neurons,6 motor outputs,157 total;54×156=**8,424** parameters. Sensor rows and self-diagonal weights are zero; all other connections are present, with their values inherited identically by the four family members. Initialize independent normal weights with **standard deviation 1/sqrt(156)**, using the same initial seed-key split as es_init. No extra bias parameters: use the existing bias observation. Keep the same observations, initial zero recurrent state, two clamp/tanh microticks, action sampling, action efference copy, signalling, world physics, energy and lifetime length. Development keys remain in the evaluator interface/world schedule but do not randomize inherited direct weights.
- **Weight invariant and limits:** Direct weights are finite, raw and unbounded, and remain bit-identical for the entire lifetime; no birth projection, weight update, predictor update, decay, growth or pruning. Plastic weights are clipped to±3, so amplitude constraints differ. Record initial/final maximum absolute direct weight and fraction with absolute value>3; saturation may help or hurt. The comparison changes parameterization, density, developmental variation and search dimension (8,424 versus155), so equal ES budget does not mean equal optimization difficulty or isolate plasticity as the sole causal factor. A direct-baseline loss cannot establish that recurrence without weight changes cannot learn.
- **Fixed training:** Three independent seeds0,1,2; startblank; defaultCfg;200 generations; population128;8 training worlds/generation; antithetic centered-rank ES, sigma0.1, learning rate0.1, Adam and L2=0.005 unchanged. Match A's settings, not B's different optimizer settings. Retain the exact ES random-key splitting so training-world keys for a given evolution seed/generation match A's schedule despite the new genome length. Checkpoint every10; resume retains the saved controller and all original settings. Reject changed controller, wrong-size or nonfinite checkpoint vectors before writing any artifact.
- **Evaluation fixed:** At generation200 only, evaluate stationary and flip150 conditions with the same EVALv3 seed2026111901,256 world-families, reference policies, raw encounter/survival arrays, bin/meal metrics and world SE. A0/A1/A2 comparators are the immutable E112 raw arrays, paired by evolution seed0/1/2 and evaluation world. No development or tuning on those held-out worlds. Development fixtures use seed41 and tiny worlds. Reversal is a secondary diagnostic; stationary fitness is the sole primary decision metric. Report all three seeds individually and preserve failure/unfinished states; do not select the best baseline or a favorable condition.
- **Gate declared now:** For each pair i, compute per-world A_i minus direct_i fitness, its mean delta_i and sample SD/sqrt256 SE_i. Continue beyond Phase2 **only if all three** delta_i > 2*SE_i after all three baselines complete the fixed budget and pass validity checks. Otherwise stop for owner input and headline the affected comparison: a direct advantage beyond two paired SE is evidence favoring the baseline; a difference inside that uncertainty is **unresolved**, not proven equivalence. No practical equivalence margin is claimed. Report any point-estimate baseline win and its uncertainty plainly. No majority exception or post-result hyperparameter repair. These errors quantify worlds for fixed genomes, not variation across trained seeds.
- **Validity gate:** Require finite source/initial/checkpoint/final genomes, correct explicit format/defaultCfg/budget, clean recorded source, contiguous generations0..199, valid resumptions, identical source-start bytes and final-checkpoint mean, complete final event data and counts/fitness reconciliation. The direct final weights must pass the fixed-lifetime invariant tests; the prior155-number path and EVALv1 must remain exact on this machine. Failure/timeouts/guard stops remain preserved; a bounded interrupted training segment is not a scientific result and receives no invented final SE.
- **Exact initial command for seed0 (benchmark within its registered training):** `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --name E113-direct-s0 --controller direct-rnn-v1 --eval-protocol direct-v1 --condition-split final --start blank --no-plastic --generations 200 --seed 0 --pop 128 --worlds 8 --sigma 0.1 --lr 0.1 --eval-worlds 256 --checkpoint-every 10 --max-train-seconds 120`. This measures initial cold/warm generation timing without exposing final evaluation; stop after a complete generation at the bound, checkpoint, inspect resources and estimate total cost before continuing. The supervisor full-process limit for this segment is600seconds.
- **Exact continuation template:** `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --resume runs/<the recorded E113-direct-sN folder> --max-train-seconds 3600`. Resolve the one immutable folder for the matching seed from the initial command; never create a replacement resume directory. Each continuation supervisor has a3900second full-process limit, below3hours. Preserve metric prefixes and checkpoint provenance.
- **Exact initial seed1/seed2 commands after feasibility is verified:** the seed0 command above with `--name E113-direct-s1 --seed 1 --max-train-seconds 3600`, then `--name E113-direct-s2 --seed 2 --max-train-seconds 3600`; all other arguments identical. Run sequentially, completing/auditing each before starting the next. Use continuation template if bounded segments stop before200. No retry changes after a failed scientific gate.
- **Software verification/budget:** The pre-change baseline is E112's91 passing local tests; hostedCI44404157089 is running and must complete before new local heavy compute. Add direct encoding/channel/recurrence/fixedweights/key-schedule tests and real tiny ES split/resume/flushed-tail regression plus protocol/provenance validation. A delegated compute worker owns released tests and training, BelowNormal, D:TEMP/TMP, fresh startup physical/availablecommit/C:/D: floors8/10/10/10GiB and runtime commit/C:/D:3/5/10GiB. One heavy job at a time, no service stops, no dependency change. Recorded local guarded compute through Phase1 is845.7567957seconds today; account for hostedCI and all subsequent tests/segments, and ask before exceeding about8hours. No final run begins until implementation/preregistration are committed and relevant checks pass.

## E114 preregistration · 2026-11-19 · EVAL v4 individual paired diagnostics
- **Authority and scope:** Owner explicitly answered “Approve E114 diagnostics” to the published proposal, then delegated scientific decisions to the council while going to sleep. Council decisions may now replace requests for owner research decisions during that absence; every changed protocol still requires prior registration and committed verification. This does not relax laptop, storage, service, privacy or daily compute limits. E113's continuation gate remains FAIL. E114 includes no evolution, reward change, mechanism addition or capability-promotion gate.
- **Hypothesis to examine, not assume:** High aggregate direct-controller fitness can coexist with poor matched individual performance under opposite hidden rules. A family mixing opposite fixed specialists can also conceal individual failure behind rule-group averages. These are descriptive diagnostics, not causal tests of dependence on experience. Privileged greedy is an explicit counterexample to interpreting successful outcomes alone as learning.
- **Fixed sample:** EVAL v4 uses seed2026111902, 256 independent base worlds, default Cfg, four agents,300steps. Split the seed into world and development streams as in EVALv3; retain all256 world/development keys. Each base world runs initialrule0 and initialrule1, each stationary and with a flip before eating atstep150. Pair grid, positions, agent index/development and random streams at the start. Subsequent trajectories may diverge; do not force later physical states to match. Save initial grid/positions and keys, all binary event arrays, per-agent returns and paired outcomes.
- **Fixed saved controllers:** A0/A1/A2 are the unchanged final genomes at runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0, runs/20261112-050319_E103-A-blank-s1, runs/20261112-102755_E103-A-blank-s2. Direct0/1/2 are the unchanged final genomes at runs/20261119-033803_E113-direct-s0, runs/20261119-040602_E113-direct-s1, runs/20261119-041609_E113-direct-s2. Use each folder's genome_final.npy, validate its encoding and source provenance, copy exact source bytes into the new run and record SHA256 before evaluation. A retains both existing lifetime updates; direct weights remain fixed. No structural growth, mutation or optimizer execution.
- **Fixed references, evaluated once each:** fixed_A, fixed_B, mixture, random_walk and greedy. Specialists use the existing local greedy navigation and tie/safe-fallback semantics while treating one fixed color as food and the other as undesirable, without accessing the real rule; preferences never flip. Mixture assigns agentindices0/1 to A and2/3 to B, fixed across every replay. Random walk is uniform across the same five actions. Ordinary greedy alone knows the current rule, including immediately after reversal. All references have zero communication and identical world physics/energy/death/regrowth/action PRNG semantics. These references are behavioral counterexamples, not independently evolved seeds.
- **Per-condition measurements:** Preserve fitness with world-family SE, food/poison counts, ordinal meals and50-step all-agent/survivor bins from the existing summary helper; explicitly retain deaths and zero-encounter agents. Report survival immediately before step150 and at the end. Undefined accuracy remains null for no encounters. Survival/return use all initial agents, including those that died before reversal. Arrays retain each individual's paired returns, minimum stationary return and joint survival; distributions are descriptive, not separate replicates.
- **Five fixed paired metrics per controller:** stationary_fitness is the mean over both stationary rules and all four agents within each baseworld; individual_min_return takes the minimum of the two stationary returns separately for each individual, then averages the four; joint_survival averages the indicator of each individual being alive at both stationary endpoints; post_flip_return averages food-minus-poison duringsteps[150,300) over both reversed lives and all initial agents; reversal_final_survival averages terminal survival over both reversed lives and all initial agents. Overall reversal fitness is also descriptive. Each SE is sample SD of the256 baseworld values divided bysqrt256, never an agent-iid or512-rule-world SE. Zero post-flip return may reflect early death and must be interpreted with death/encounter counts.
- **Fixed contrasts:** For each i=0,1,2 and each of the five paired metrics above, calculate A_i minus direct_i within each baseworld, then its mean and paired-world SE:15 prespecified contrasts total. Keep every world, including worlds with no survivors/encounters. These correlated estimates are descriptive; no multiple-testing claim, selected best seed, numerical capability threshold, promotion criterion or post-result contrast selection. Reference metrics are reported side by side without inventing extra significance tests.
- **Validity gate:** PASS only if all11controllers×4conditions complete with finite return/survival/SE values, correct keys/starting-state/development pairing, fixed specialist assignments, unchanged source genome bytes, correct preflip equivalence, consistent binary event/death/encounter accounting, complete requested per-agent outputs, reproducible policies and all statistics reconstructing from raw events. Null accuracy is valid only when the denominator is zero. A timeout, memory/disk guard stop, missing output, wrong source or inconsistent accounting is incomplete/failed measurement, preserved and reviewed before anything else. This is not a capability gate and cannot overturn E113.
- **Exact final command:** `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --name E114-v4 --eval-protocol v4 --condition-split final --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256`. Fixed manifest and finalseed above; no --allow-dirty, --quick, resume, controller/start override, training bound, config override or changed update flags. One tracked run stores each completed condition's raw evidence once, updates an explicit partial summary, and never resumes over a previous attempt. Each completed controller's wall time is recorded; the first is the benchmark within the total budget, not an extra scientific sample.
- **Verification:** Existing unchanged baseline is112 passing local/hosted tests at implementation9db7fafe (current docs/resultHEAD2e216d5). New fixtures use seed41 and tiny worlds only. Test hand-calculated paired uncertainty and mixture counterexample; all-starting-agent death/no-encounter handling; identical initial states/keys/development and stationary/reversal preflip events; fixed reference assignments and equivalence to existing greedy/random physics; saved direct/plastic engine event equivalence; source/protocol validation; partial failure preservation and resume rejection. - **Interpretation:** A has positive individual minima and about71–73% joint stationary survival across fresh matched rules, while direct genomes have minima near−4 and almost no joint survival. The mixture has high average fitness but negative individual minima and zero joint survivors, demonstrating why group averages cannot stand for individual cross-rule competence. These are descriptive estimates over independent base worlds, not15 undeclared significance tests. The same initial individual is replayed in fresh lives; lifetime weights/state are not carried between them. Privileged greedy succeeds without learning, so these outcomes do not prove dependence on experience. A post-flip return remains negative in3/3, and E113 remains FAIL.
- **Resources/budget:** sampledpeakprivate0.982707977GiB,mincommit10.913883209GiB,C:free21.881973267GiB,D:free46.232898712GiB. Diagnostic used248.9981698/1800seconds. Local verification used353.1673363/900seconds, leaving546.8326637 for hostedCI. Measured supervised daily total3994.2282865seconds before hostedCI; minor read-only audit overhead is not assumed zero. No scientific job remains active.
- **Asked:** distinguish individual performance across opposite rules from the success of specialist populations.
- **Happened:** all11panels valid; A individual minima8.237±0.302,8.453±0.310,8.396±0.297SE versus direct near−4; mixturemean33.486±1.236 but individualminimum−3.970±0.008 and no joint survivors.
- **Means:** the new diagnostic exposes a repeatable aggregation failure and a stationary cross-rule performance difference in the saved models.
- **Does not mean:** new learning occurred during development of this diagnostic, the old failed gate passed, or reversal/causal learning/useful connection growth was established.
- **Next:** publish the complete diagnostic and verify hostedCI; council reviews a separately preregistered objective-only continuation study under the owner delegation. No such training is part of E114.

## E115 preregistration · 2026-11-19 · Objective-only continuation of existing plastic learners
- **Council decision under owner delegation:** Both the independent paper/research chat and skeptical reviewer recommend a bounded training comparison after E114 exposed the specialist-mixture failure. Continue the existing A learners rather than introduce another biological mechanism or substitute a cheaper direct-only question. E113 remains FAIL, and E114 is a diagnostic, not a capability promotion. The coordinator selects the stricter all-three gate proposed by the research chat, with the reviewer's starting-genome and joint-survival safeguards, before any E115 training. No threshold or budget will be selected from E115 scientific outcomes.
- **Hypothesis:** Selection on the worse stationary-rule return of each starting individual improves the existing plastic controllers' matched cross-rule return more than selection on mean return, at identical exposure and continuation budget. This is stationary robustness in existing learners, not discovery from blank, reversal recovery, causal proof of learning or superiority to a direct controller trained under the new objective.
- **Only experimental difference:** paired-mean versus paired-min aggregation. For every candidate, replay each of four base worlds under both stationary rules with identical world/development/action streams and fresh lifetime state. Mean takes the average of the two returns per individual, then averages individuals/worlds; min takes each individual's lower return first, then averages. Both include all starting agents, including deaths. World physics, observations/actions, architecture, both lifetime updates, structural-off mode and ES are identical. Never compare against historical unpaired E103 as if it isolated the objective: both new arms receive the same eight family lifetimes per candidate.
- **Fixed starting points and random streams:** Each pair starts from the same original A0/A1/A2 genome_final.npy bytes registered in E114 (paths below), preserving the155-number format. Start with new zero Adam moments, step0, and training seeds1000,1001,1002 respectively in both arms. These namespaces were not found in prior experiment registrations. Existing es_init/es_step schedules generate identical mutation/world/development streams between paired arms; optimized means may diverge. No learned lifetime state is inherited between worlds. The starting source bytes, hashes and clean source provenance must be recorded and verified on every initial run/resume.
- **Fixed budget/configuration:** default Cfg,50 generations per arm, population64, four base worlds each replayed under both rules, sigma0.1,lr0.1,L2=0.005, existing antithetic sampling/rank shaping/Adam unchanged. Six continuations total, not six new initial pretraining runs. Checkpoint every10 generations. Never select a best checkpoint: evaluate generation50 only after all six valid training runs finish. Training summaries explicitly defer held-out evaluation; no EVALv1/v3/v4/v5 test is called at training start, interruption or completion.
- **Exact common training arguments:** `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol paired-train-v1 --condition-split final --generations 50 --pop 64 --worlds 4 --sigma 0.1 --lr 0.1 --checkpoint-every 10 --eval-worlds 256`, followed by exactly one row's arguments below. Execution order is mean0 benchmark/continuation, min0, mean1, min1, mean2, min2; no overlapping heavy jobs.
  - `--name E115-mean-s0 --objective paired-mean --seed 1000 --start file:runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0/genome_final.npy --max-train-seconds 120`
  - `--name E115-min-s0 --objective paired-min --seed 1000 --start file:runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0/genome_final.npy --max-train-seconds 1800`
  - `--name E115-mean-s1 --objective paired-mean --seed 1001 --start file:runs/20261112-050319_E103-A-blank-s1/genome_final.npy --max-train-seconds 1800`
  - `--name E115-min-s1 --objective paired-min --seed 1001 --start file:runs/20261112-050319_E103-A-blank-s1/genome_final.npy --max-train-seconds 1800`
  - `--name E115-mean-s2 --objective paired-mean --seed 1002 --start file:runs/20261112-102755_E103-A-blank-s2/genome_final.npy --max-train-seconds 1800`
  - `--name E115-min-s2 --objective paired-min --seed 1002 --start file:runs/20261112-102755_E103-A-blank-s2/genome_final.npy --max-train-seconds 1800`
- **Exact continuation command:** `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --resume runs/<the one recorded folder for that canonical E115 name> --max-train-seconds 1800`. Resolve and record the unique existing folder, preserve its starting bytes, original target and metric prefix, and reject changed objective/settings or a completed run. Failed/crashed/interrupted attempts remain; no replacement initial run or cherry-picked restart. Initial benchmark has a600-second full-process cap; subsequent training invocations have2100-second full-process caps, below3hours.
- **Feasibility before releasing the remaining study:** The mean0 initial segment is part of its fixed50 generations, not an extra model. Measure cold/warm generation times and memory, check checkpoint/provenance, and estimate all remaining generations plus setup, evaluation and verification. Runtime/resources alone determine feasibility. Do not lower population, generations or otherwise repair scientific settings from training/evaluation scores. If the fixed study cannot fit the budgets, preserve the segment and record a new prospective council decision before any further execution; no incomplete scientific claim.
- **Fresh evaluation, separately versioned EVAL v5:** After all six generation50 finals pass validity, run `.venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 scripts/run_experiment.py --name E115-v5 --eval-protocol v5 --condition-split final --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256`. Use new seed2026111903, defaultCfg256 base worlds, the same four paired stationary/reversal conditions and metrics as v4. Fixed manifest: unchanged starts0/1/2, mean0/1/2 and min0/1/2 final genomes from the unique six registered training folders, plus fixed_A,fixed_B,mixture,random_walk,greedy once each:14 panels. Source hashes/paths/provenance are recorded before evaluation; no intermediate or best-genome selection. Preserve all raw event, individual and paired outputs. Do not reuse EVALv4 worlds for development or tune on this new seed. V4's public protocol and legacy behavior remain unchanged.
- **Fixed30 descriptive contrasts:** For each seed, min-minus-mean and min-minus-unchanged-start for each of the five v4 paired metrics (stationary mean return, individual minimum return, joint stationary survival, post-flip return, reversal final survival). Calculate the difference within each matched baseworld and its sample-SD/sqrt256 SE; keep every starting agent/world. Report original per-rule fitness, survival, encounters/poison counts and distributions regardless of the gate. No additional selected contrasts or multiple-comparison-adjusted significance claim.
- **Numerical continuation gate fixed now:** PASS only if (1) all six training runs finish50 generations with valid source/finite checkpoint/history and all14 final panels pass measurement validity; (2) in **each of all three seeds**, min-trained individual minimum return exceeds **both** its paired-mean final and its unchanged starting A genome by strictly more than two paired-world SE; and (3) no seed's min-minus-start joint-survival contrast is below minus two paired-world SE. Otherwise FAIL: improvement under this fixed continuation budget is not established. The starting safeguard prevents a degraded control or trivial zero-return avoidance from counting as progress. This is an operational research gate, not a causal learning threshold or corrected hypothesis test. Stationary success cannot count as passing the randomized-reversal rung; reversal is reported separately, even if it worsens.
- **Verification and cost cap:** Baseline133 local E114 tests passed; E114 hostedCI is still running and must terminate before any local heavy check. Software fixtures use tiny worlds and seed41 only, not the final training/evaluation settings. Verify correct aggregation order, both arms' exact exposure/key schedules and initial zero moments, real split/resume/flushed-tail replay, strict source/objective/checkpoint validation and no held-out calls during training; preserve exact legacy/v4 behavior; test all-six/evaluation manifests, raw evidence protection, and hand-calculated gate failures including a degraded control and survival regression. Commit verified code/preregistration before training. E115 has a prospective aggregate cap of **14400seconds (four hours)** of supervised local/hosted compute including verification, benchmark, training and final evaluation, while remaining below the overall eight-hour daily limit. Final evaluation has a1200-second full-process cap; reserve verification/evaluation time in every feasibility projection. No automatic budget extension or setting change.
- **Laptop/publication:** One low-priority heavy job, D:temporaryfiles, unchanged startup physical/commit/C:/D:8/10/10/10GiB and runtime commit/C:/D:3/5/10GiB floors; no service stops, installs or downloads. Root coordinates releases; compute worker executes/guards/audits; independent council reviews results. Commit/push source, every completed or interrupted run, generated RESULTS, README and append-only LOG under Winter-Soren. Record the actual gate plainly, then council decides the next separately registered action under the owner's delegation.

### E115 gate decision · 20 November 2026 · FAIL

**Question.** Can selection on each starting individual's lower return across two stationary food rules improve an existing plastic controller? Each of three original A genomes started two 50-generation continuations under identical exposures. We evaluated the original, mean-selected and minimum-selected genomes together in 256 fresh paired world families.

**Result.** Recovery 1 completed all 14 panels. From its summary, the three minimum-selected genomes scored 7.216 ± 0.293, 6.011 ± 0.276 and 6.753 ± 0.273 on individual minimum return. Their differences versus matched mean-selected finals were +0.284 ± 0.404, −0.611 ± 0.362 and +0.436 ± 0.381. Their differences versus unchanged starts were −1.475 ± 0.406, −2.234 ± 0.427 and −1.828 ± 0.403. Each difference uses one paired-world standard error. All six required superiority checks failed. Joint-survival differences versus starts were −0.025 ± 0.020, −0.050 ± 0.021 and −0.081 ± 0.020. Only the first met the survival safeguard. Min-selected post-flip returns were −2.040 ± 0.085, −2.037 ± 0.084 and −2.225 ± 0.086. All 30 prespecified contrasts, 56 conditions and evidence links appear in results/EVAL_V5.md.

**Interpretation.** Every unchanged start scored above both of its continued descendants on worse-rule return. Changing the aggregation failed to overcome that deterioration. All six trained genomes collected fewer food items in the paired stationary lives than their starts. Causal attribution to a specific optimizer setting or a lifetime learning mechanism remains open.

**Recovery and validity.** Source 39a85927acd4b39e43840e993b6d9044fe74d55c, runs/20261120-060829_E115-v5-recovery1. The runner completed in 464.594 seconds. The external guard exited successfully after 477.2912099 seconds, with minimum available commit 14.2498 GiB and minimum free disk 17.2975 GiB on C and 46.2135 GiB on D. Total E115 supervised wall charge 14096.6330259/18000 seconds, including the full interrupted interval. Today's recovery used 477.2912099 seconds. The original interrupted folder and all 54 fingerprints are intact. The independent audit reconstructed the scientific results from 70 archives and confirmed exact agreement across 624 overlapping arrays, 35 condition summaries and eight paired summaries. The recovery does not increase the number of independent worlds. Reproduction on this checkout:

    .venv/Scripts/python.exe -X utf8 tools/audit_e115.py --evaluation runs/20261120-060829_E115-v5-recovery1 --interrupted-manifest results/diagnostics/E115-interrupted-files.json

**Decision.** Retain the original three A genomes as the starting benchmarks. E113 and E115 keep their failed gate decisions. The council recommends a prospective matched learning-rate comparison under the same stationary world physics. The proposed E116 study is awaiting the owner's failed-gate decision. No E116 job has started.

### E116 preregistration · 21 November 2026 · Size of evolutionary updates

The owner asked us to continue after reviewing the E116 proposal and to reuse prior learning. E116 reuses the original three E103 genomes. It compares evolutionary learning rates 0.1 (control) and 0.02 (candidate) with paired-min selection, from fresh zero Adam moments. Both arms inherit the same genome per seed. Within-life weights reset at birth. The separately requested environmental parameters will be developed under ecology-v1 and remain disabled in this comparison.

Hypothesis: the smaller step improves worse-rule return beyond both the original genome and its matched control while preserving joint survival. EVAL v6 evaluates the generation-20 finals and original starts on 256 new paired base worlds (seed 2026112001), with four conditions per panel and the same five references as v5. The 30 contrasts consist of candidate minus control and candidate minus start for all five v5 paired measures, in all three seeds. For each seed, both minimum-return differences must exceed two paired world standard errors strictly, and candidate-minus-start joint survival must be at least minus two paired standard errors. All six finals and 14 panels must pass validity. Any other valid completed result fails. Incomplete evidence leaves the scientific decision open. No best checkpoint selection or intermediate held-out evaluation. Report original outcomes, survival, counts and reversal regardless.

Commit the code and this registration before execution. Full local software suite and hosted CI share a 2,400-second reserve. The total cap is 10,800 seconds, including the 1,200 seconds reserved for final evaluation. Training and setup share 7,200 seconds. Startup floors for physical memory, commit headroom and both drives are 8, 10, 10 and 10 GiB respectively. Runtime floors for commit and C/D are 3, 5 and 10 GiB. One low-priority job, temporary files on D, and no more than eight hours for the day. Record benchmark wall, slowest warm generation and startup/compile overhead. Before releasing the remaining work, budget 1.5 times the slowest measured warm generation plus every expected setup and full reserves. Stop if infeasible. No automatic retries or relaxation of thresholds.

### Engineering verification completed · 21 November 2026 · Persistence after a setback

The disabled and enabled A0 runs used clean source 2db4fe6d3b96f6a09624689d636222df6fedc4a6. Their supervisors finished in 12.193 and 11.974 seconds respectively. The focused persistence, lineage and runner suites passed 7/7, 2/2 and 7/7 tests. Both groups reached shelter with one of their two newborns, both with the trait enabled and with it disabled. All assigned creatures survived. Across the three enabled cohorts the shared-memory group had one tactic attempt and one actual action change at that attempt. The fresh-weight group had two attempts and one action change. The groups' first-cohort experiences are paired and identical. A changed sampled action after an attempt does not by itself establish that the bias caused it.

Decision: the development check passed. The trait changes action preferences using only locally available cues, and the recorded behavior can be inspected. The underlying genome was fixed. These are one-seed engineering observations with no estimable between-lineage error or scientific performance claim. A future scientific study would need fresh independent lineages, matched cold-experienced donors and identical recipient worlds.
