### E117 preregistration · 21 November 2026 · Can newborns inherit faster shelter finding?

The owner approved a cold donor versus fresh-start comparison for all three original evolved A genomes. Real-world outcome data for this protocol have not been opened. The hypothesis is that sharing one donor cohort's acquired sensory-to-motor weight changes lets outside-born newborns reach shelter sooner on an unseen world, without a measurable survival loss. All three independent evolution seeds must meet both thresholds below. The thresholds apply separately to each seed.

For each genome, draw 256 independent donor–recipient world families with four assigned creatures per cohort. The two lives have independently drawn worlds and development keys. In each family the donor and the recipients face the same stationary food rule, alternating by family index to give 128 families per rule. Both lives use the default 300-turn grid, with shelter observations, a movement energy cost of 0.01 and an outdoor cold cost of 0.02 starting at turn 151. Use the existing plasticity rules. Keep the optional persistence trait off in both arms. Hold the original genome, newborn wiring, starting positions, world, and action random stream fixed across arms. Reset bodily and neural activity at birth. In one branch initialize the newborns' existing connections with the donor archive. In the other use fresh starting weights. Include all donors, including their last living weights if they die. Clear the archive between families. Do not train or select genomes.

For recipients born outside shelter, record the first turn (1–300) ending alive inside. Assign 301 to every non-arrival, including death on attempted entry. Births inside shelter have latency zero, are reported separately and are excluded from the primary mean. The primary difference is fresh minus inherited mean latency. Calculate each arm and the paired difference over all outside births, with each independent family as one uncertainty cluster. For world numerator x, count n and ratio r = sum(x)/sum(n), use the sample SD of x − r n, divided by sqrt(number of worlds) and the mean n. For the paired difference use the paired world numerator. For survival use all four assigned recipients and the paired world difference. Gate for each lineage: the latency difference must exceed two paired standard errors and survival (inherited minus fresh) must be at least minus two paired standard errors. Only a pass in all three lineages establishes the specified milestone. Otherwise report failure, including the sign and uncertainty of each estimate. No change to the gate after viewing results.

Report arrivals/outside births, survival/all assigned, food minus poison and cold shelter occupancy among all assigned cold agent-turns, along with outdoor cold exposures, donor deaths and evidence that acquired weights reached newborns. Birth-to-entry can precede cold. Even a pass could reflect changed foraging or exploration. Identifying shelter-specific content requires a separate matched archive control.

Use Python from the project's environment, a committed clean scientific source and the following exact runner arguments. All three final seed values are fresh. Source file SHA-256 digests for A0, A1 and A2 respectively are `477d9d03c713dcab8072349ff9155813e5a384d3709eff67595a342b55111f53`, `35196cd30c2de5c1ed5bb9e23ce445ff6ad13bd1a33bdbf7a77f54d53edce899` and `f299069753636a303f6ba7f91be78099e8491b20a7511bdcef47fd8b4c5bbaa6`.

```powershell
python scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol cold-donor-v1 --name E117-benchmark --condition-split development --seed 42 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 4 --start file:runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0/genome_final.npy
python scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol cold-donor-v1 --name E117-A0 --condition-split final --seed 2026112101 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --start file:runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0/genome_final.npy
python scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol cold-donor-v1 --name E117-A1 --condition-split final --seed 2026112102 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --start file:runs/20261112-050319_E103-A-blank-s1/genome_final.npy
python scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol cold-donor-v1 --name E117-A2 --condition-split final --seed 2026112103 --generations 0 --eval-worlds 256 --start file:runs/20261112-102755_E103-A-blank-s2/genome_final.npy
```

Benchmark first on development worlds at full life length. Run one guarded below-normal laptop process at a time. Wall caps are 600 seconds for the benchmark and 9,900 seconds for each final invocation, subject to eight hours' cumulative compute per day. Preserve incomplete runs. Before interpreting any final result, finish the three panels and independently reconstruct the outcomes and paired uncertainties from the raw family events. Update the README and results index with the verdict.

### E118 preregistration · 21 November 2026 · Can selection evolve faster shelter finding?

The owner explicitly requested that the genomes evolve. This design was fixed before reading any E117 result. It tests whether ten generations of selection for living shelter arrival improve each of the three independently evolved A genomes. Preserve the three original parents. Use the genotype after generation ten without selecting a checkpoint. Keep food, poison, cold exposure, costly movement, within-life plasticity, and the programmed persistence trait active throughout both training and testing in the default ecology grid (movement cost 0.01, cold cost 0.02). Each life starts from its own freshly developed wiring with an empty archive. This experiment measures inherited changes in the 155-number genome. E117 separately measures transfer of acquired weight changes.

Use the existing rank-based antithetic evolution strategy, population 16, four new training worlds per generation, paired positive/negative perturbations, noise scale 0.05 and learning rate 0.02. Measure each initially outside creature's first turn ending alive inside shelter, with 301 for death or nonarrival by turn 300. Training rewards are 301 minus that turn, averaged over outside births per world; a world with no outside births contributes zero. There is no shelter-entry reward for death on arrival. Selection uses training worlds only. Keep all eleven genomes per lineage, from the unchanged parent through the ten updates, plus the training records.

On 128 independent unseen worlds for each original lineage, alternate food rules, match the parent and descendant's environment and development random keys, and allow the different genomes to produce different wiring. Separately record births inside shelter. Pool capped first-entry times over outside births; use world-clustered ratio standard errors as for E117. The paired primary difference is parent minus descendant in turns. The survival difference is descendant minus parent including every assigned newborn. A seed passes only if its travel-time improvement exceeds two paired standard errors and the survival difference is at least minus two paired standard errors. All three must pass for a replicated improvement. Retain the previous reference if the gate fails. Report every direction and uncertainty, including negative outcomes. The gate measures this task with the fixed trait. A change in genome values is not itself a gain in ability.

A0/A1/A2 source paths and exact SHA-256 values are those registered in E117. The development run uses training seed 43 and evaluation seed 44. The three training seeds are 2026112201, 2026112202 and 2026112203. Their three evaluation seeds are 2026112301, 2026112302 and 2026112303. Use one below-normal local job at a time and fresh evaluation keys. Limit the benchmark to 900 seconds, each final panel to 3,600 seconds and all work to the existing daily compute allowance. Preserve interrupted attempts. These are the commands:

    python scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol shelter-evolution-v1 --name E118-benchmark --condition-split development --seed 43 --generations 1 --pop 16 --worlds 4 --sigma 0.05 --lr 0.02 --eval-worlds 4 --start file:runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0/genome_final.npy
    python scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol shelter-evolution-v1 --name E118-A0 --condition-split final --seed 2026112201 --generations 10 --pop 16 --worlds 4 --sigma 0.05 --lr 0.02 --eval-worlds 128 --start file:runs/20261112-023245_E103-A-blank-s0/genome_final.npy
    python scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol shelter-evolution-v1 --name E118-A1 --condition-split final --seed 2026112202 --generations 10 --pop 16 --worlds 4 --sigma 0.05 --lr 0.02 --eval-worlds 128 --start file:runs/20261112-050319_E103-A-blank-s1/genome_final.npy
    python scripts/run_experiment.py --eval-protocol shelter-evolution-v1 --name E118-A2 --condition-split final --seed 2026112203 --generations 10 --pop 16 --worlds 4 --sigma 0.05 --lr 0.02 --eval-worlds 128 --start file:runs/20261112-102755_E103-A-blank-s2/genome_final.npy
Council review fixed two interpretation limits before running. First living arrivals can precede cold, so report the number before cold and the fraction of assigned cold turns ending alive in shelter. Ten generations with four training worlds per generation is a bounded test of this search budget. A failed gate does not exclude a better policy or a different training budget.

### E117 result · 21 November 2026 · Donor experience reduced shelter latency in all three lineages

All three preregistered gates passed. In A0, A1 and A2 order, capped first-living-entry latency averaged 161.609 ± 4.503, 160.038 ± 4.113 and 145.233 ± 4.297 turns when fresh, and 123.213 ± 3.837, 115.431 ± 3.757 and 107.891 ± 3.276 with inherited experience. The paired latency gains were 38.397 ± 5.297, 44.606 ± 5.368 and 37.341 ± 5.376 turns. Paired all-assigned survival gains were 8.984 ± 1.202, 10.742 ± 1.518 and 6.836 ± 1.002 percentage points. Each estimate uses 256 independent world families per lineage. Arrival counts among outside births were 726/960 versus 543/960, 759/955 versus 544/955 and 793/958 versus 607/958 (inherited versus fresh). The 64, 69 and 66 births inside shelter, respectively, remained in the survival denominators. The archive changed the starting weights of all 1,024 assigned newborns in each lineage. The donors included 453, 453 and 424 individuals with living outdoor cold exposure. All original death and nonarrival records were retained.

Food minus poison also increased (inherited 12.99 ± 0.46, 14.55 ± 0.44, 11.32 ± 0.33; fresh 4.64 ± 0.28, 5.45 ± 0.33, 4.46 ± 0.23). Mean fractions of assigned cold turns spent alive inside shelter were 0.0526 ± 0.0044, 0.0542 ± 0.0041, 0.0596 ± 0.0042 when inherited, and 0.0169 ± 0.0018, 0.0180 ± 0.0020, 0.0234 ± 0.0026 when fresh. Most inherited first arrivals occurred before cold. Transfer helped successors in this ecology. The donor archive can also encode food or movement experience, so shelter-specific knowledge remains unresolved. Absolute end survival with inheritance was 12.21 ± 1.09%, 15.92 ± 1.31% and 7.71 ± 0.95%. The independently registered E118 evolution test proceeds without changing this verdict.

The separate event audit finished in 32.324 seconds and checked 982 recorded file hashes. It verified the preserved 181-family prefix from the interrupted A2 attempt and the 75 newly evaluated families, the genome and measurement-source identities, matched newborns, raw living events, archive arithmetic and all three decisions. All 768 matched families were scored once. The recovery supervisor completed in 109.772 seconds with the native Windows memory fallback used once. The recovery code is 4a271c2d03f3f5c9c5d57011e4b0523503fd0776; the original scientific source is 95a254bcbe5c35c5f8ec343e056c579d562645ac. E117 supervised compute including the final audit was approximately 1,691 seconds. Reproduce the audit with:

    python tools/audit_cold_donor.py --source-commit 95a254bcbe5c35c5f8ec343e056c579d562645ac --a2-recovery runs/20261121-181108_E117-A2-recovery

### E118 result · 21 November 2026 · Genomes evolved; the three-seed performance gate failed

The held-out comparison finished on 128 independent world families per lineage (384 total). All original parents and generation-ten descendants used the same persistence rule, empty archive, ecology and development/world random streams within each paired evaluation. Outside births numbered 483, 477 and 470 of 512 assigned newborns per arm. All three originals completed ten ES updates; the independent checker reconstructed each update from its candidates, fitness, perturbations and optimizer state. Eleven saved genome vectors per lineage underlie the figure. Genome displacement measures movement in raw parameter space.

In A0/A1/A2 order the parent mean capped latency was 141.990 ± 5.817, 157.704 ± 5.870 and 147.343 ± 5.648 turns. Descendants scored 148.615 ± 5.957, 166.187 ± 6.142 and 142.353 ± 5.773. Paired turns saved were −6.625 ± 6.049, −8.482 ± 6.383 and +4.989 ± 6.901. None exceeded two paired standard errors. End survival changes were 0.000 ± 0.920, −0.586 ± 1.223 and +0.391 ± 1.073 percentage points. All three survival safeguards passed. Parent living arrivals were 317/483, 281/477 and 296/470; descendants reached 295/483, 263/477 and 301/470. Of the descendant arrivals, 275, 239 and 281 occurred before cold. Descendant cold occupancy over all assigned cold turns was 0.02979 ± 0.00411, 0.01986 ± 0.00340 and 0.03145 ± 0.00479; corresponding parent values were 0.02669 ± 0.00367, 0.02949 ± 0.00500 and 0.03332 ± 0.00458.

The registered replicated improvement gate failed. Preserve the original parents as references and all final descendants as evidence. This outcome bounds the tested budget and selection procedure. It does not overturn E117's paired benefit from acquired donor weights. A matched scrambled archive would address whether the contents of transferred experience matter; no follow-on scientific comparison is authorized by this result alone.

### E119 preregistration · 21 November 2026 · Can an evolving descendant use inherited experience to beat its own parent?

The owner requested local training of all three lineages with acquired experience transmitted across evolutionary generations. No E119 worlds have been evaluated. Start with the actual generation-ten endpoints of E118, even though that experiment failed its improvement gate. Retain the original references. Restore each endpoint's complete evolution-strategy state, including its genome, Adam moments, generation counter and RNG key. The three checkpoint SHA-256 digests in A0/A1/A2 order are 2e1bea556060085e34c2dc7cf027f639d3f36b4ee592c64b1135e8c09907a4d2, 1984cacb45e87a3fdc29bb03bb83a424873f333619008086ca98b0c768a2e78e and d0c8ce66eba91dba04151b70347ba7eae084ee9e35616ed2dd43d0a81883a740. The development run continues the single E118 benchmark update, using checkpoint 9c8fa76b3b2b3da22707199cf00988621d873f5901dcd45c7115354b87286cde. E118 had no inherited archive, so the starting archive is empty for every lineage.

Continue ten evolutionary updates under the same shelter objective, world, plasticity, persistence, population, noise scale, learning rate and four training worlds as E118. Before each update, the current population-mean parent lives on four additional independent training-only donor worlds, with four agents in each and both food rules represented equally. Each donor starts with its own genome-developed wiring and the archive that parent inherited. For each sensory-to-output connection, sum its last living strength minus its actual starting strength over the 16 donors and divide by the number that possessed it. Include zero changes and deaths. Add the resulting change to the parent's archive and clip to the existing weight bound. Each offspring candidate receives that new archive on the connections it possesses, with the same clipping. Keep all other newborn state fresh. Selection scores earlier living shelter arrival in the registered E118 manner. Save all eleven local genome/optimizer/archive states and all ten donor updates per lineage. The four added donor worlds supply experience; their outcomes do not directly determine selection. No evaluation-world experience enters training or the archive.

After the ten updates, evaluate the final descendant (global generation 20) against its immediate parent (generation 19) on 128 fresh world families per lineage. The parent receives the archive it actually inherited at birth and the descendant receives its own. Use paired world/development random keys, alternating food rules, fresh newborns and no sharing across evaluation families. Also evaluate the parent with the descendant's latest archive, the descendant with the previous archive, the descendant with an empty archive, and the original E118 endpoint with its actual empty archive. These six matched arms distinguish the complete descendant from its immediate parent and its founder, and quantify how much the trained descendant benefits from inherited weights or just the latest archive update. They do not isolate the effect of using archives during training versus an equally trained no-inheritance line, or identify what information the archive contains.

The primary measure is the parent minus descendant difference in pooled time to the first turn ending alive in shelter. Exclude inside births from latency only, count death/nonarrival as 301, and include everyone in end survival. Use world-level paired ratio standard errors for latency and paired world standard errors for survival. Each lineage passes only if the latency improvement is strictly greater than two paired standard errors and descendant-minus-parent survival is at least minus two paired standard errors. All three must pass for the replicated parent-beating claim. Report the identical contrast against the E118 founder separately, regardless of the primary outcome. For the empty-archive, previous-archive and matched-latest-archive comparisons report paired latency and survival with their uncertainty. A gain against the founder or an archive control cannot substitute for a failed immediate-parent comparison. Do not choose between checkpoints using held-out worlds.

E119 donor master seeds are 2026112401, 2026112402 and 2026112403. Held-out masters are 2026112501, 2026112502 and 2026112503. Development uses donor seed 45 and evaluation seed 46.
